# Swedish search demand and keyword universe for Verdana (Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central consultancy), Sweden, October 2026

## 1. Which real Swedish queries do autocomplete services return?

### Takeaway
Swedish searchers mix English product names with Swedish category and buying words. Google recognises Swedish commercial phrases such as "business central konsult", "business central pris", "business central licenser", "business central implementering", "business central support", "vad kostar business central" and "byta affärssystem". It also links product and category through "business central affärssystem", "affärssystem business central" and "navision affärssystem". Technical and migration topics are searched in English ("nav to business central migration", "navision 2018 end of life", "business central shopify connector"). The required seed "business central eller" returns no comparison suggestions, because comparisons are typed with "vs". Most of the Swedish long-tail phrasings named in the task return no suggestions on any engine.

### Cited Findings

#### Method and provenance
- **Main run.** On 2026-10-11, between 13:48 and 14:06 CEST, 607 queries per engine were sent with curl in Bash to the Google, Bing and DuckDuckGo autocomplete endpoints.
  - Query set: 37 seeds from the task's minimum list, about 190 extended seeds, 47 question seeds, 25 long-tail tests and 10 brand seeds.
  - Alphabet expansions (seed + space, a–z, å, ä, ö = 30 queries each) for 11 seeds: business central, dynamics 365 business central, business central partner, business central pris, navision, dynamics nav, affärssystem, affärssystem för, erp system, byta affärssystem and verdana.
  - Every one of the 607 seeds has a successful (HTTP 200) response from each engine.
  - Throttling: each engine was first queried sequentially with a 0.6 s pause between requests (about 3.5–4 s per request in practice). The rest ran from curl config files at `--rate 50/m` per engine.
  - Lists were deduplicated per seed and engine, giving 5,118 unique suggestions across engines.
- **Supplementary runs (Google and Bing only):**
  - 49 seeds (98 requests) at 14:15–14:17 CEST, `--rate 40/m`.
  - 12 seeds for the brief's insight topics (24 requests) at 14:49–14:50 CEST, `--rate 30/m`. One of these repeated a main-run seed and is listed only once.
  - All supplementary requests returned HTTP 200.
- **Endpoint patterns:** [Google](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central), [Bing](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=business%20central&mkt=sv-SE), [DuckDuckGo](https://duckduckgo.com/ac/?q=business%20central&kl=se-sv&type=list). Section 8 lists every seed with links to its own requests.
- **Coverage.** Google returned no suggestions for 145 of the 607 main-run queries; Bing and DuckDuckGo for 115 each (computed from the responses linked in section 8).
- **DuckDuckGo is not independent evidence.** Its list was a subset of Bing's for 606 of the 607 seeds; the one exception differs only by a character-encoding error. Example: [DuckDuckGo "business central pris"](https://duckduckgo.com/ac/?q=business%20central%20pris&kl=se-sv&type=list) vs [Bing "business central pris"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=business%20central%20pris&mkt=sv-SE).
- **Bing leaks other markets.** Bing's sv-SE market mixes Norwegian, Danish, Dutch and German queries into thin Swedish seeds:
  - "vad kostar business central" returned "hvad koster business central", "hva er business central" and "was kostet business central im monat" ([Bing "vad kostar business central"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=vad%20kostar%20business%20central&mkt=sv-SE)).
  - "verdana ö" returned oil-product brands and Florida place names such as "verdana oils" and "verdana estero fl" ([Bing "verdana ö"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=verdana%20%C3%B6&mkt=sv-SE)).
- **Google pads thin seeds.** Google fills thin Swedish seeds with English or foreign completions:
  - "nav support" gave "nav support phone number" and "gps support garmin" ([Google "nav support"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=nav%20support)).
  - "business central rådgivning" gave "business central support …" ([Google "business central rådgivning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20r%C3%A5dgivning)).

#### Product and category head terms
- "business central" alone completes to product and navigational queries: business central 365, dynamics 365, login, dynamics, microsoft, erp, mcp, api, logo ([Google "business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central)).
- "dynamics 365 business central" completes to licensing and administration queries: premium, essentials, support, team members, login, admin center, essentials price, device users ([Google "dynamics 365 business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=dynamics%20365%20business%20central)).
- Swedish product–category bridges:
  - "business central affärssystem" is #3 for "business central a" ([Google "business central a"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20a)).
  - "affärssystem business central" is #3 for "affärssystem b" in Google and #8 in Bing ([Google "affärssystem b"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20b), [Bing "affärssystem b"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20b&mkt=sv-SE)).
  - "navision affärssystem" is #2 for "navision" and #1 for "navision " ([Google "navision"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision), [Google "navision "](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20)).
  - "erp system business central" is #2 for "erp system b" ([Google "erp system b"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20system%20b)).
- "business central sverige" is an exact Google suggestion. Its completions include "business central svenska" (#3), "business central partner sverige" (#5) and "microsoft business central sverige" (#6) ([Google "business central sverige"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20sverige)). "business central partner sverige" is also #3 for "business central partner s" ([Google "business central partner s"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20partner%20s)).
- "business central återförsäljare" (reseller) is #1 for both "business central å" and "business central grossist" ([Google "business central å"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20%C3%A5), [Google "business central grossist"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20grossist)).

#### Commercial and service queries
- Exact-match (echo) Google suggestions:
  - "business central konsult" is #1, with "business central konsult lön" at #2 ([Google "business central konsult"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20konsult)).
  - "business central pris" is #1 ([Google "business central pris"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris)).
  - "business central licenser" is #2 for "business central licens"; #1 is "business central licensing guide 2026" ([Google "business central licens"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20licens)).
  - "business central kostnad" ([Google "business central kostnad"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20kostnad)).
  - "business central implementering" is #1, followed by English implementation partner, cost and guide queries ([Google "business central implementering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20implementering)).
  - "business central support" ([Google "business central support"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20support)) and "business central utvecklare" ([Google "business central utvecklare"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20utvecklare)).
- Swedish price variants:
  - "business central essentials pris" is #3 for "business central pris e" ([Google "business central pris e"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20e)).
  - "dynamics 365 business central premium pris" is #1 for "business central pris p" ([Google "business central pris p"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20p)).
  - "microsoft business central pris" is #3 for "business central pris m" ([Google "business central pris m"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20m)).
  - "dynamics 365 business central pris" is an echo ([Google "dynamics 365 business central pris"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=dynamics%20365%20business%20central%20pris)).
  - Bing adds "business central priser" and "microsoft business central priser" ([Bing "business central pris"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=business%20central%20pris&mkt=sv-SE)).
- English price and licensing long tail:
  - Google maps "business central pris per användare" to "business central price per user" ([Google "business central pris per användare"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20per%20anv%C3%A4ndare)).
  - "business central price increase october 2025" ([Google "business central pris i"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20i)) and "business central essentials vs premium" ([Google "business central essentials"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20essentials)).
- Training:
  - "business central utbildning" returns "business central kurs" as #1 in Google ([Google "business central utbildning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20utbildning)).
  - Bing returns "kurs i business central" and "kurser i business central" for dozens of seeds, e.g. [Bing "vad kostar business central"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=vad%20kostar%20business%20central&mkt=sv-SE).
- Integration and development queries are English:
  - integrations and api ([Google "business central integration"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20integration), [Google "business central api"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20api))
  - shopify connector ([Google "business central shopify"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20shopify))
  - edi integration ([Google "business central edi"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20edi))
  - power automate ([Google "business central power automate"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20power%20automate))
  - al development ([Google "business central al"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20al))
  - extensions ([Google "business central extension"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20extension))
- Swedish user-problem queries:
  - "business central driftstörning" (#1) and "business central driftstatus" (#2) for "business central drift" ([Google "business central drift"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20drift)).
  - "business central sie export" is #1 for "business central sie" ([Google "business central sie"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20sie)).

#### Comparisons and the required seed "business central eller"
- "business central eller", a required seed, returned no comparison suggestions on any engine. Google offered only "central business park ellerslie", and Bing and DuckDuckGo returned navigational queries such as login, logo and admin center ([Google "business central eller"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20eller), [Bing "business central eller"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=business%20central%20eller&mkt=sv-SE)).
- Comparisons are typed with "vs":
  - Google: "business central vs dynamics 365 / navision / finance and operations" ([Google "business central vs"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20vs)).
  - Bing adds sage, sap, netsuite, odoo and quickbooks ([Bing "business central vs"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=business%20central%20vs&mkt=sv-SE)).
  - "business central vs dynamics nav" and "dynamics nav vs business central" are echo queries with many variants ([Google "business central vs dynamics nav"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20vs%20dynamics%20nav), [Google "dynamics nav vs business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=dynamics%20nav%20vs%20business%20central)).

#### Swedish phrasings with no suggestions on Google, Bing or DuckDuckGo
- Migration and upgrade wording: "nav uppgradering" ([Google "nav uppgradering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=nav%20uppgradering)), "navision uppgradering" ([Google "navision uppgradering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20uppgradering)), "navision support upphör" ([Google "navision support upphör"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20support%20upph%C3%B6r)), "migrera nav" ([Google "migrera nav"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=migrera%20nav)) and "nav migrering" ([Google "nav migrering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=nav%20migrering)).
- Selection and roll-out wording: "nytt affärssystem" ([Google "nytt affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=nytt%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)), "implementering affärssystem" ([Google "implementering affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=implementering%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)), "införa affärssystem" ([Google "införa affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=inf%C3%B6ra%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)) and "införande av affärssystem" ([Google "införande av affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=inf%C3%B6rande%20av%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)).
- Service and segment wording: "business central förvaltning" ([Google "business central förvaltning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20f%C3%B6rvaltning)), "business central tjänsteföretag" ([Google "business central tjänsteföretag"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20tj%C3%A4nstef%C3%B6retag)) and "al utveckling" ([Google "al utveckling"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=al%20utveckling)).
- Cloud wording: "business central molnet" ([Google "business central molnet"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20molnet)) and "affärssystem i molnet" ([Google "affärssystem i molnet"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20i%20molnet)).
- Fortnox wording: "business central fortnox" ([Google "business central fortnox"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20fortnox)) and "business central vs fortnox" ([Google "business central vs fortnox"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20vs%20fortnox)).
- From the second supplementary batch (Google and Bing only), nothing on either engine for "migrering affärssystem" ([Google "migrering affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=migrering%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)), "erp utmaningar" ([Google "erp utmaningar"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20utmaningar)), "utmaningar affärssystem" and "vanliga erp utmaningar" ([Google "vanliga erp utmaningar"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vanliga%20erp%20utmaningar)).

#### Swedish phrasings that Google answers with English or other wording
- "uppgradera navision" → "upgrade navision" ([Google "uppgradera navision"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=uppgradera%20navision)).
- "dynamics nav uppgradering" → "dynamics nav upgrade (to business central)" ([Google "dynamics nav uppgradering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=dynamics%20nav%20uppgradering)).
- "migrera nav till business central" → "migrate nav to business central" ([Google "migrera nav till business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=migrera%20nav%20till%20business%20central)).
- "business central migrering" and "business central uppgradering" → English migration and upgrade lists ([Google "business central migrering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20migrering), [Google "business central uppgradering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20uppgradering)).
- "navision till business central" → completions without "till": "navision business central", "navision vs business central", "navision to business central upgrade" ([Google "navision till business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20till%20business%20central)).
- "business central och power bi" and "business central och copilot" → "och" is dropped, leaving English-modified lists such as connector, reports, apps, license, agents and credits ([Google "business central och power bi"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20och%20power%20bi), [Google "business central och copilot"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20och%20copilot)).
- "erp migrering" → "erp-migrering" (Google only; [Google "erp migrering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20migrering)).

#### Category queries
- "affärssystem för" completes to: tillverkande företag (#1), förkortning, hantverkare, byggbranschen, konsultföretag, småföretag, byggföretag, e handel, företag, konsulter ([Google "affärssystem för"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20f%C3%B6r)).
- "affärssystem" alone completes to: och tjänstedesign (a study programme), engelska, sap, ifs, monitor, pyramid, m3, erp, bilverkstad ([Google "affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem)). "affärssystem bäst i test" is #2 for "affärssystem b" ([Google "affärssystem b"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20b)).
- "erp system" completes to: betyder, meaning, examples, exempel, ifs, sap, monitor, microsoft, visma ([Google "erp system"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20system)).

### Inferences
- The Swedish commercial vocabulary is small and specific. Titles should combine the English product name with a Swedish buying word (pris, licenser, konsult, implementering, support) and the category word "affärssystem".
- Bing and DuckDuckGo presence is weak evidence of Swedish demand because of market leakage. Google suggestions containing Swedish words are the most reliable autocomplete signal.
- Migration and technical pages need English variants (end of life, migration, upgrade, connector) in H2s, FAQ items and body copy, because that is how Swedish searchers phrase those topics.
- Comparison content should use "vs" in titles and headings ("Business Central vs Navision"), not "eller".
- Phrases below the autocomplete threshold can still appear in natural copy, but they should not be primary targets.

### Gaps
- Autocomplete reflects popularity order, not volume. An empty list means "below threshold", not zero searches.
- The data covers one day (2026-10-11) and one network location, and Google may localise suggestions by location.

## 2. Which questions do Swedish searchers ask?

### Takeaway
Real Swedish question wording centres on "vad är", "vad kostar", "vilket … är bäst" and "att tänka på". Examples:
- "vad är business central (för system)" and "vad kostar business central"
- "är business central / navision ett erp system"
- "vad är ett affärssystem", "vad kostar ett affärssystem / erp system" and "vad står erp system för"
- "vilket affärssystem är bäst" and "vilka affärssystem finns det"
- "byta affärssystem att tänka på"

Swedish "hur", "när" and "varför" questions about ERP return no Google suggestions, including the brief's own wording "när bör man byta affärssystem". Google's People Also Ask boxes could not be collected.

### Cited Findings
- "vad är business central" completes to "vad är business central för system" (#2), "vad är microsoft business central", "vad är dynamics 365 business central" and "vad kostar business central" ([Google "vad är business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20%C3%A4r%20business%20central)). "business central vad är" completes to "business central vad är det" ([Google "business central vad är"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20vad%20%C3%A4r)).
- "vad kostar business central" is an exact suggestion ([Google "vad kostar business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20kostar%20business%20central)).
- ERP status questions:
  - "är business central ett erp system" is #5 for "business central ä" ([Google "business central ä"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20%C3%A4)).
  - "är navision ett erp system" is #1 for "är navision" ([Google "är navision"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=%C3%A4r%20navision)).
  - "vad är navision" is an echo in both Google and Bing ([Google "vad är navision"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20%C3%A4r%20navision), [Bing "vad är navision"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=vad%20%C3%A4r%20navision&mkt=sv-SE)).
- "vad är ett affärssystem" completes to "hur är ett affärssystem uppbyggt" (#3), "vad kostar ett affärssystem", "vad är erp affärssystem" and "vad gör ett affärssystem" (#9). It also returns vendor-specific forms such as "vad är ifs / m3 / pyramid affärssystem" and "vad är affärssystem sap" ([Google "vad är ett affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20%C3%A4r%20ett%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)).
- "vad kostar ett affärssystem" is an exact suggestion, followed by "vad kostar pyramid affärssystem" and "vad kostar monitor affärssystem" ([Google "vad kostar ett affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20kostar%20ett%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)). "vad kostar ett erp system" is #2 for "vad kostar erp" ([Google "vad kostar erp"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20kostar%20erp)).
- "vad är erp" completes to "vad är erp system", "vad är erp förkortning för", "vad är erp projekt", "vad är erp konsult" and "vad är erp-lösningar". It also returns therapy-related noise ("vad är erp behandling", "vad är erp ocd") ([Google "vad är erp"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20%C3%A4r%20erp)).
- "vad är ett erp system" completes to "vad gör ett erp system", "vad kostar ett erp system" and "vad står erp system för" ([Google "vad är ett erp system"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vad%20%C3%A4r%20ett%20erp%20system)).
- "vilket affärssystem" completes to "vilket affärssystem är bäst" and "vilka affärssystem finns det" ([Google "vilket affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=vilket%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)). "byta affärssystem" completes to "byta affärssystem att tänka på" ([Google "byta affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=byta%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)).
- Bing-only questions: "är erp och affärssystem samma sak" ([Bing "erp affärssystem"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=erp%20aff%C3%A4rssystem&mkt=sv-SE)) and "vad heter affärssystem på engelska" ([Bing "affärssystem h"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20h&mkt=sv-SE)).
- Google returned no suggestions for:
  - "när ska man byta affärssystem" ([Google "när ska man byta affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=n%C3%A4r%20ska%20man%20byta%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)) and the brief's wording "när bör man byta affärssystem" ([Google "när bör man byta affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=n%C3%A4r%20b%C3%B6r%20man%20byta%20aff%C3%A4rssystem))
  - "varför byta affärssystem" ([Google "varför byta affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=varf%C3%B6r%20byta%20aff%C3%A4rssystem))
  - "hur lång tid tar det att byta affärssystem" ([Google "hur lång tid tar det att byta affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=hur%20l%C3%A5ng%20tid%20tar%20det%20att%20byta%20aff%C3%A4rssystem))
  - "skillnad mellan navision och business central" ([Google "skillnad mellan navision och business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=skillnad%20mellan%20navision%20och%20business%20central))
  - "hur fungerar business central" ([Google "hur fungerar business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=hur%20fungerar%20business%20central)) and "hur väljer man affärssystem" ([Google "hur väljer man affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=hur%20v%C3%A4ljer%20man%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)); Bing returned only unrelated or foreign queries for both
  - "fördelar med business central" ([Google "fördelar med business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=f%C3%B6rdelar%20med%20business%20central)) and "business central nackdelar" ([Google "business central nackdelar"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20nackdelar))
- Technical questions are recognised in English, e.g. "business central go live checklist" ([Google "business central go live"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20go%20live)). "what is navision" is a rising related query in Google Trends (section 3).
- Bing's related searches for "vad kostar business central" were "Business Central pris", "microsoft Business Central pris", "microsoft Business Central" and "Business Central abonnemang" ([Bing SERP](https://www.bing.com/search?q=vad+kostar+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
- NAB's ERP page FAQ uses "Vad är ett ERP-system?", "Vad är Business Central?", "Varför ska jag ha ett ERP-system?" and "Vilka typer av företag använder Business Central?" ([NAB](https://nabsolutions.com/se/erp/)).
- People Also Ask: Google's results page answered with an "unusual traffic" verification page, so PAA was not collected, and no attempt was made to bypass it ([attempted Google SERP](https://www.google.com/search?q=business+central+pris&hl=sv&gl=se&pws=0)). The Bing results pages viewed showed no PAA-style question block in the extracted text.

### Inferences
- FAQ sections should reuse these exact wordings. Predicted questions ("hur lång tid tar …", "när ska man byta …") are still useful content, but they should be marked internally as predicted and not used as primary keywords.
- "Business Central abonnemang" deserves a mention as a synonym on the pricing pages.

### Gaps
- No People Also Ask data from Google. AlsoAsked and AnswerThePublic require accounts and were not used.
- No Swedish forum threads (e.g. Flashback) were found. A web search aimed at forum discussion returned vendor pages only, so buyer-forum wording is missing.

## 3. How do Swedes name the category and products?

### Takeaway
"Affärssystem" is the dominant Swedish category word. Over five years in Swedish Google Trends it averaged about 1.7× "erp system" and about 12× "ekonomisystem". "ERP" appears mostly in definitional and English queries. "Business Central", often with "Microsoft" or "Dynamics 365", is the product name. "Navision" has almost vanished as a search term but survives in migration searches; "business central navision" is rising. "NAV" and "BC" on their own are ambiguous. Correct Swedish compounds take a hyphen ("ERP-system", "Business Central-partner"), while users type "erp system" and "e handel" without one.

### Cited Findings
- Google Trends, Sweden, past five years, weekly data from the week of 10–16 Oct 2021 to the (incomplete) week of 11–17 Oct 2026, relative interest ([Google Trends](https://trends.google.com/trends/explore?date=today%205-y&geo=SE&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem,erp%20system,ekonomisystem,business%20central,navision&hl=sv); values read from the page's own data response on 2026-10-11):

  | Term | 5-year average | Last 12 months | Weeks with data (of 262) |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | business central | 42 | 59.8 | 249 |
  | affärssystem | 24 | 27 | 233 |
  | erp system | 14 | 15.3 | 181 |
  | ekonomisystem | 2 | 4.6 | 29 |
  | navision | 1 | 1.2 | 21 |

- Trends related queries (same source). "Breakout" appears as "Tydlig ökning" in the Swedish interface.
  - "erp system". Top related queries are definitional and English-heavy: erp meaning (100), erp system meaning (97), monitor erp (72), vad är erp system (69), erp system betyder (68). Rising: business central (breakout) and microsoft dynamics 365 (breakout).
  - "navision". Top: microsoft navision (100), navision dynamics (95), business central navision (54). Rising: business central navision (+450%), business central (+350%), what is navision (+150%).
  - "business central". Top: microsoft business central (100), dynamics business central (98), dynamics (97), business central 365 (84). Rising: shopify business central (breakout), business central appsource (breakout), dynamics 365 business central for iws (breakout), business central status (+300%), business central erp (+80%).
  - "affärssystem". Top: pyramid (100), ifs (68), affärssystem och tjänstedesign (53), sap (49), affärssystem engelska (45), visma affärssystem (32), fortnox (26). Rising: affärssystem för tillverkning (breakout).
- "ekonomisystem" completions point to finance-only, smaller or public-sector buyers: medelstora företag, raindance, visma, småföretag, fastighetsbolag ([Google "ekonomisystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=ekonomisystem)). Its top Trends related queries are briljant ekonomisystem and agresso ekonomisystem (same Trends source).
- "verksamhetssystem" is a public-sector term: lifecare, socialtjänsten, linköpings kommun ([Google "verksamhetssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verksamhetssystem)).
- Users type both "erp system" and "erp-system": Google suggests "erp-system exempel", "erp-systemet" and "erp-system betyder" ([Google "erp system"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20system), [Google "erp-system"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp-system)). "affärssystem e-handel" completes to "affärssystem e handel" ([Google "affärssystem e-handel"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20e-handel)).
- Swedish style rules from Språkrådet:
  - A compound whose first part is an abbreviation is written with a hyphen (e-post, p-piller), unless it has become an established word (ubåt) ([Språkrådet Frågelådan](https://frageladan.isof.se/visasvar.py?svar=50952)).
  - When a compound contains a multi-word expression, a hyphen goes before the last word ("prova på-dagar") ([Språkrådet Frågelådan](https://frageladan.isof.se/faqs/20742)).
- Competitor wording:
  - "Business Central-partner" ([Modia](https://modia.se/), [BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/))
  - "ERP-partner" ([InBiz](https://inbiz.se/))
  - "affärssystem (ERP)" in NAB's H1 ([NAB](https://nabsolutions.com/se/erp/))
  - "ERP-system" in ads ([Bing SERP "erp system"](https://www.bing.com/search?q=erp+system&setlang=sv&cc=SE))
- "implementering" vs "implementation":
  - Both "business central implementering" and "business central implementation" are recognised ([Google "business central implementering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20implementering), [Google "business central implementation"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20implementation)).
  - "implementera affärssystem" and "erp implementering" are exact suggestions ([Google "implementera affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=implementera%20aff%C3%A4rssystem), [Google "erp implementering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20implementering)).
  - "implementering affärssystem", "införa affärssystem" and "införande av affärssystem" return nothing (section 1).
- Cloud wording:
  - "molnbaserat affärssystem" and "molnbaserat ekonomisystem" are exact suggestions ([Google "molnbaserat affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=molnbaserat%20aff%C3%A4rssystem), [Google "molnbaserat ekonomisystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=molnbaserat%20ekonomisystem)).
  - "affärssystem i molnet" and "molnbaserat erp" return nothing ([Google "affärssystem i molnet"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20i%20molnet), [Google "molnbaserat erp"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=molnbaserat%20erp)).
  - English "business central cloud", "cloud vs on premise" and "cloud migration" are recognised ([Google "business central cloud"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20cloud), [Google "business central on premise"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20on%20premise)).
- Abbreviation ambiguity:
  - Short "nav" completions mix in Hungary's NAV tax authority ("nav gov hu ügyfélkapu belépés", [Google "navision g"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20g)), the rapper Nav ("nav rapper", [Google "navision r"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20r)) and Norway's NAV ("nav ikt support", "nav support utdanning", [Bing "nav support"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=nav%20support&mkt=sv-SE)).
  - "bc partner" completes to the private-equity firm BC Partners ([Google "bc partner"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=bc%20partner)), and "bc konsult" returns noise ([Google "bc konsult"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=bc%20konsult)).
- The misspelling "affärsystem" (one s) is answered with "affärssystem" suggestions ([Google "affärsystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rsystem)).

### Inferences
- Use "affärssystem" as the primary category word and pair it with "ERP" in headings ("affärssystem (ERP)", "ERP-system"). Use "ekonomisystem" only as a secondary synonym for finance-led buyers, and avoid "verksamhetssystem".
- Use "Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central" once near the top of key pages, then "Business Central". Never use "BC" alone in titles or H1s.
- Write "Dynamics NAV (Navision)" on first mention, use "Navision" in Swedish copy and "NAV 2016/2017/2018" for versions. Avoid a bare "NAV" in titles unless it sits next to a version number or "Dynamics".
- Write correct Swedish in visible copy (ERP-system, Business Central-partner, e-handel). That Google also matches the unhyphenated variants users type is an assumption this research did not test.
- Use "implementering" in Swedish headings with "implementation" in body text, and prefer "molnbaserat affärssystem" over "affärssystem i molnet".

### Gaps
- Google's treatment of hyphen vs space in query matching was not tested.
- No Trends comparison of "implementering" vs "implementation"; both words are too generic for Trends to be useful.

## 4. What is the relative demand and priority per cluster?

### Takeaway
Measurable demand in Google Trends exists only for the head terms: business central (growing), affärssystem and erp system. Every service-modifier query tested (partner, pris, konsult, support, integration) is below Trends' reporting threshold in Sweden: a thin but high-value long tail. For a new entrant, the top priorities are:
- the Business Central hub (product name plus the "affärssystem" bridge)
- NAV/Navision migration (time-critical, with weak Swedish-language competition)
- pricing and licensing
- implementation
- consultant and partner intent

### Cited Findings
- Head-term Trends data: see section 3 ([Google Trends](https://trends.google.com/trends/explore?date=today%205-y&geo=SE&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem,erp%20system,ekonomisystem,business%20central,navision&hl=sv)).
- Service terms in Trends, Sweden, five years ([Google Trends](https://trends.google.com/trends/explore?date=today%205-y&geo=SE&q=business%20central%20partner,business%20central%20pris,business%20central%20konsult,business%20central%20support,business%20central%20integration&hl=sv)):

  | Term | 5-year average | Weeks with any data (of 262) | Last 12 months |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | business central partner | 0 | 1 | 0 |
  | business central pris | 0 | 0 | 0 |
  | business central konsult | 0 | 1 | 1.5 |
  | business central support | 0 | 0 | 0 |
  | business central integration | 0 | 3 | 0.2 |

- Ads on Bing (a signal of commercial value), 2026-10-11:
  - "vad kostar business central": NAB Solutions, Fellowmind and Cosmo Consult ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=vad+kostar+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central konsult": NAB Solutions ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+konsult&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central partner": Fellowmind ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+partner&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central implementering": Fellowmind ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+implementering&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "byta affärssystem": Spiris (the ad says Visma Spcs is becoming Spiris), Fortnox, BusinessWith and Visma Net ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=byta+aff%C3%A4rssystem&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "affärssystem": Spiris, Visma Net, Blikk and Jeeves ERP ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=aff%C3%A4rssystem&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "erp system": BusinessWith, Spiris, Visma Net and Business NXT ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=erp+system&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
- Organic results on Bing (sv/SE), 2026-10-11:
  - "vad kostar business central": Microsoft's pricing page, BrightCom's pricing page, businesscentral.se/kostnad, Microsoft's pricing overview, d365.se, företagsfinanser.se and navet.se ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=vad+kostar+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central partner": businesscentral.se/partners ("jämför och välj rätt"), bisqo.se, BrightCom, d365.se ("jämför 11 partners"), Microsoft partner pages, navet.se and activebs.com ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+partner&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central konsult": an INVID job ad ranks first, then basecloud.se, consiliumtech.se, enqore.se, doal.se, dynamicskonsult.se, thinknine.se, CGI and triplecore.se/jobb ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+konsult&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central implementering": businesscentral.se ("Business Central-implementation: faser, tid och roller"), Microsoft Learn (English), several English partner blogs and brainaccounting.se ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+implementering&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "uppgradera navision till business central": Microsoft Learn (English), navisionplanet.com (English), NAB, 365vanguard (English), Volt Technologies (English) and navet.se ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=uppgradera+navision+till+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "business central support": Microsoft Learn help pages, Microsoft support, update.se, mdcnordic.com and the Dynamics community ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+support&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "byta affärssystem": affarssystemguiden.se ("Byta affärssystem 2026 — Steg-för-steg guide …") and pector.se ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=byta+aff%C3%A4rssystem&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "affärssystem": sv.wikipedia and a Fortnox guide ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=aff%C3%A4rssystem&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "erp system": affarssystemguiden.se and Oracle Sverige ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=erp+system&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
- Competitor content targeting:
  - BrightCom has a pricing page (H1 "Pris på Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central i Sverige", H2 "Vad kostar Business Central?") ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/dynamics-business-central/priser/)), an NAV upgrade article ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/artiklar/uppgradera-dynamics-nav)) and an integrations page ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/business-central-integrationer)).
  - Modia's sitemap lists knowledge-base articles on these topics ([Modia sitemap](https://modia.se/sitemap.xml)):
    - vad kostar ett affärssystem
    - affärssystem för tillverkande företag, e-handel, konsultföretag, and lager och logistik
    - "så vet du om det är dags att byta affärssystem"
    - "hur väljer du rätt business central partner"
    - a guide to molnbaserat affärssystem
  - NAB's sitemap lists 28 URLs whose slugs contain "erp-for-" or "affarssystem-for-". They include seven industry hubs such as /se/branscher/erp-for-grossister/ and /se/branscher/erp-for-processindustrin/ ([NAB sitemap](https://nabsolutions.com/se/sitemap_index.xml)), plus the article "Är det dags att byta affärssystem?" ([NAB](https://nabsolutions.com/se/ar-det-dags-att-byta-affarssystem/)).
  - The exact-match domain businesscentral.se presents itself as a buyer-side guide ("Business Central i Sverige – kostnad, kalkylator och partners") ([businesscentral.se](https://businesscentral.se)).
  - affarssystem.se belongs to Update Affärssystem AB, which sells Business Central and NAV ([affarssystem.se](https://affarssystem.se)).
- Fortnox markets a package titled "Affärssystem för större bolag och koncerner" (title seen in web search results) ([Fortnox](https://www.fortnox.se/paket/affarssystem)).
- NAV lifecycle dates (Microsoft lists them in Pacific Time):
  - NAV 2016 extended support ended 15 Apr 2026 ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/lifecycle/products/dynamics-nav-2016)).
  - NAV 2017 extended support ends 12 Jan 2027 ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/lifecycle/products/dynamics-nav-2017)).
  - NAV 2018 extended support ends 12 Jan 2028 ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/lifecycle/products/dynamics-nav-2018)).

### Inferences

#### Demand tiers used here (estimates; no search volumes are invented)
- **T1, measurable:** Google Trends has data for Sweden in most weeks.
- **T2, thin but real:** Google suggests the Swedish query (as an echo or a completion), but it is below Trends' threshold.
- **T3, English-mapped:** the Swedish phrasing is absent or mapped to English, while the English phrasing is recognised.
- **T4, unproven:** no suggestions on any engine.

#### Keyword clusters (deliverable 2)

| # | Cluster | Representative queries | Intent | Demand | Priority | Owner page |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | Product and category bridge | business central affärssystem, affärssystem business central, dynamics 365 business central, microsoft business central sverige, vad är business central (för system), är business central ett erp system | Commercial investigation and informational (the bare head term is mostly navigational) | T1 head, T2 bridges | High | /business-central |
| C2 | Consultants, partners, resellers | business central konsult(er), business central partner sverige, business central återförsäljare, erp konsult(er sverige), affärssystemkonsult, hyra konsult, inhyrd konsult | Commercial and transactional, with job-seeker noise | T2 | High | homepage, /tjanster/konsulttjanster, /insikter/valja-business-central-partner |
| C3 | Price and licensing | business central pris / priser / kostnad / licenser, vad kostar business central, essentials pris, premium pris, price per user, licensing guide 2026, price increase, vad kostar ett affärssystem, "Business Central abonnemang" (Bing related search) | Commercial investigation | T2 + T3 | High | /insikter/vad-kostar-business-central, /insikter/business-central-licenser |
| C4 | Implementation | business central implementering / implementation, implementera affärssystem, erp implementering, go live checklist, data migration | Commercial | T2 + T3 | High | /tjanster/implementering, go-live article |
| C5 | NAV/Navision migration and lifecycle | navision business central (Google's completion for "navision till business central"), navision affärssystem, vad är navision, är navision ett erp system, business central vs navision / dynamics nav, nav to business central migration, navision 2016/2017/2018 end of life, nav 2018 extended support, navision support, erp-migrering | Commercial and informational | T2 + T3 | High (time-critical) | /tjanster/navision-till-business-central plus two articles |
| C6 | Switching and selection | byta affärssystem (att tänka på), välja affärssystem, erp rådgivning, kravspecifikation affärssystem, upphandling affärssystem / erp, vilket affärssystem är bäst, vilka affärssystem finns det, jämför affärssystem, byta ekonomisystem | Commercial investigation | T2 | Medium-High | /tjanster/radgivning plus two articles |
| C7 | Integrations and development | business central integration(s), api, shopify connector / integration, edi integration, bank reconciliation, crm integration, power automate, power platform, al development, extensions, business central utvecklare | Commercial (technical) | T3, plus T2 for "utvecklare" | Medium | /tjanster/integrationer-och-utveckling, Shopify article |
| C8 | Support, optimisation, application management | business central support, dynamics 365 business central support, business central hjälp (Bing), driftstörning / driftstatus, performance issues, navision / dynamics nav support, systemförvaltning(smodell) | Commercial (existing users) | T2 for support and driftstörning; T4 for ERP-specific "förvaltning" | Medium | /tjanster/support-och-optimering, /tjanster/forvaltning, driftstörning and SIE articles |
| C9 | Reporting and Power BI | power bi business central (connector, apps, templates, license), business central reporting, power bi konsult | Commercial | T3 for BC-specific queries; T2 for generic "power bi konsult" | Medium-Low | /tjanster/rapportering-och-power-bi |
| C10 | AI and Copilot | copilot business central (agent, license, price, on premise), business central copilot features / credits, business central ai agents | Informational turning commercial | T3 (Swedish phrasing T4) | Medium-Low | /tjanster/ai-och-automation, /insikter/copilot-i-business-central |
| C11 | Industry and segment | affärssystem för tillverkande företag, affärssystem för e handel, affärssystem för konsultföretag, affärssystem grossister / lager / lagerhantering, affärssystem tjänsteföretag / projektredovisning, affärssystem för små / mindre / medelstora företag | Commercial investigation | T2 (Trends breakout: "affärssystem för tillverkning") | Medium for tillverkning and e-handel; Low-Medium for grossist and tjänsteföretag | /losningar/* |
| C12 | Category education | affärssystem, vad är ett affärssystem, vad gör ett affärssystem, hur är ett affärssystem uppbyggt, erp system betyder, vad står erp system för, är erp och affärssystem samma sak (Bing), ekonomisystem | Informational | T1 / T2 | Low-Medium | /insikter/vad-ar-ett-affarssystem |
| C13 | Comparisons | business central vs navision / nav / dynamics 365 / finance and operations / sap / netsuite; nothing for "business central eller" or vs fortnox / visma / monitor / jeeves | Commercial investigation | T2 / T3 (Swedish-system comparisons T4) | Low-Medium (except BC vs NAV, which belongs to C5) | /insikter/business-central-vs-navision |
| C14 | Training | business central kurs, kurs(er) i business central (Bing), dynamics 365 business central kurs | Informational and transactional | T2 | Low unless Verdana sells training | a section within the implementation and support pages |
| C15 | Exclude (noise and navigational) | business central login / logga in / download / logo / status, jobs / lön, AL function names, Norwegian and Hungarian NAV, "erp" in the therapy sense, verksamhetssystem, BC Partners, Fortnox/Visma small-business switching | n/a | n/a | Exclude | none, except the driftstörning help article |
| C16 | Brand | verdana (the typeface), verdana business central / konsult / affärssystem | Navigational | Name collision | High (defensive) | homepage, /om-verdana |

#### Reasoning per cluster
- **C1.** "business central" has the most Trends interest and is growing (42 → 59.8), but the bare term is navigational: login, status and api ([Google "business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central)). Microsoft and the product itself will own it. The winnable part is the Swedish bridge queries ([Google "business central a"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20a), [Google "affärssystem b"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20b), [Google "business central sverige"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20sverige)). Priority High: this is the hub every other page links to.
- **C2.** These are bottom-of-funnel queries.
  - All sit below Trends' threshold ([Trends](https://trends.google.com/trends/explore?date=today%205-y&geo=SE&q=business%20central%20partner,business%20central%20pris,business%20central%20konsult,business%20central%20support,business%20central%20integration&hl=sv)), but advertisers pay for "business central konsult" and adjacent terms.
  - Part of the "konsult" intent comes from job seekers: "business central konsult lön" ([Google "business central konsult"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20konsult)), and an INVID job ad ranks first on Bing.
  - Aggregators hold the "partner" results.
  - Verdana must not claim Microsoft-partner status until it is verified, so the homepage should target "Business Central-konsulter". A neutral "how to choose a partner" article serves the partner keyword better.
- **C3.** Price and licensing have the largest Swedish autocomplete family of any service topic ([Google "business central pris"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris), [Google "business central licens"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20licens), [Google "business central pris e"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20e), [Google "business central pris p"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20p)). The competition is Microsoft, BrightCom and businesscentral.se. Verdana cannot publish its own prices. It can explain the cost components and cite Microsoft's Swedish list prices with a date ([Microsoft pricing](https://www.microsoft.com/sv-se/dynamics-365/products/business-central/pricing)).
- **C4.** Swedish echo queries exist ([Google "business central implementering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20implementering), [Google "implementera affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=implementera%20aff%C3%A4rssystem), [Google "erp implementering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20implementering)), and Bing shows few Swedish partner pages for the query, so winnability is comparatively good. This is also Verdana's main service.
- **C5.** Volume is low (navision about 1 in Trends), but buying intent and timing are excellent:
  - NAV 2017 support ends 12 Jan 2027 and NAV 2018 support on 12 Jan 2028 ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/lifecycle/products/dynamics-nav-2017), [Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/lifecycle/products/dynamics-nav-2018)).
  - "business central navision" is rising +450% in Trends.
  - English pages dominate the Swedish results ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=uppgradera+navision+till+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).

  This is the best ratio of value to competition in the universe.
- **C6.** Swedish demand is real ([Google "byta affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=byta%20aff%C3%A4rssystem), [Google "kravspecifikation affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=kravspecifikation%20aff%C3%A4rssystem), [Google "erp rådgivning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=erp%20r%C3%A5dgivning), [Google "välja affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=v%C3%A4lja%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)). However, mass-market vendors (Fortnox, Spiris, Visma) and guide sites dominate both ads and organic results, so treat this as a medium-term authority play.
- **C7.** The long tail is rich but English and technical ([Google "business central integration"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20integration), [Google "business central api"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20api), [Google "business central bank"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20bank)). Shopify is a Trends breakout. The searchers are often developers, but the queries also show which integrations buyers need.
- **C8.** "business central support" is an echo query, but Microsoft's self-help pages dominate its results. "förvaltning" has no ERP-specific demand ([Google "förvaltning affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=f%C3%B6rvaltning%20aff%C3%A4rssystem), [Google "business central förvaltning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20f%C3%B6rvaltning)), while "systemförvaltning" exists as a generic concept ([Google "systemförvaltning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=systemf%C3%B6rvaltning)). Help articles on driftstörning and SIE can attract existing Business Central users, who are support prospects.
- **C9.** BC-specific Power BI queries are English ([Google "power bi business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=power%20bi%20business%20central)). "power bi konsult" is a broad BI-consultancy market ([Google "power bi konsult"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=power%20bi%20konsult)).
- **C10.** Demand is English-only so far ([Google "copilot business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=copilot%20business%20central), [Google "business central copilot"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20copilot)), and Swedish phrasing returns nothing ([Google "ai i affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=ai%20i%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)). The topic is still worth covering: the brief requires an AI page, and the market is new.
- **C11.** Swedish buyers phrase industry needs as "affärssystem för …" rather than "business central för …" ([Google "affärssystem för"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20f%C3%B6r) vs [Google "business central för tillverkning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20f%C3%B6r%20tillverkning)). Tillverkning has the strongest signal: the #1 suggestion and a Trends breakout.
- **C12.** These are the highest-volume informational terms, but Wikipedia, Fortnox and Oracle rank for them ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=aff%C3%A4rssystem&setlang=sv&cc=SE)). They add topical depth rather than near-term traffic.
- **C13–C16.** See the table. Comparisons with the Swedish small-business systems Fortnox and Visma have no Business Central-related autocomplete ([Google "business central vs fortnox"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20vs%20fortnox), [Google "business central vs visma"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20vs%20visma)). The Fortnox switching queries are about Spiris, Visma and Bokio ([Google "byta från fortnox"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=byta%20fr%C3%A5n%20fortnox), [Google "fortnox eller"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=fortnox%20eller)).

### Gaps
- No absolute volumes. Google Keyword Planner, Semrush and Ahrefs require accounts or captchas and were not used, so priorities rest on tiers, Trends indices, autocomplete position and competition.
- Rankings were observed on Bing (sv/SE) as a proxy, because Google's results page was gated. Bing and Google rankings differ.

## 5. Which long-tail opportunities can a new entrant realistically win?

### Takeaway
The best realistic wins are Swedish or Swedish-English pages where demand evidence exists and Swedish competition is thin:
- the NAV lifecycle and migration cluster (time-critical)
- "business central implementering"
- Swedish explainers on Business Central licences and prices
- help articles that attract existing users: "business central sie export" and "business central driftstörning"
- Swedish versions of English technical queries: go-live checklist and Shopify

None of the long-tail phrases named in the task has Swedish autocomplete. Each should be rephrased to the wording searchers actually use (below).

### Cited Findings
- Quick-win candidates, with evidence:
  - **NAV lifecycle.** "navision end of life", "navision 2018 end of life", "navision 2016 end of life", "nav 2017 end of life", "nav 2018 support end" and "nav 2018 extended support" are all recognised ([Google "navision end of life"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20end%20of%20life), [Google "navision 2018"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%202018), [Google "nav 2017 end of life"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=nav%202017%20end%20of%20life), [Google "nav 2018 support"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=nav%202018%20support)). Results for the Swedish upgrade query are mostly English pages ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=uppgradera+navision+till+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - **Implementation.** "business central implementering" is an echo query ([Google "business central implementering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20implementering)). Bing shows businesscentral.se plus English pages ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=business+central+implementering&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - **SIE export.** "business central sie export" is #1 for "business central sie" ([Google "business central sie"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20sie)). Microsoft documents SIE export/import as part of the Audit File Export app in the Swedish localization ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dynamics365/business-central/localfunctionality/sweden/sweden-local-functionality)).
  - **Driftstörning.** "business central driftstörning" and "business central driftstatus" are recognised ([Google "business central drift"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20drift)), and "business central status" is rising +300% in Trends ([Google Trends](https://trends.google.com/trends/explore?date=today%205-y&geo=SE&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem,erp%20system,ekonomisystem,business%20central,navision&hl=sv)).
  - **Licences.** "business central licenser", "business central essentials pris" and "dynamics 365 business central premium pris" ([Google "business central licens"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20licens), [Google "business central pris e"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20e), [Google "business central pris p"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20p)).
  - **Go-live.** "business central go live checklist" ([Google "business central go live"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20go%20live)).
  - **Shopify.** "business central shopify connector / integration / inventory sync / b2b" ([Google "business central shopify"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20shopify)), and "shopify business central" is rising (breakout) in Trends. BrightCom's sitemap includes a Shopify integration page ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/shopify-integration-business-central)).
- Medium to hard:
  - "vad kostar business central": Microsoft, BrightCom and aggregators rank, and three advertisers bid ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=vad+kostar+business+central&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "byta affärssystem": guide sites, NAB and Modia target it ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=byta+aff%C3%A4rssystem&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
  - "affärssystem för tillverkande företag" ([Google "affärssystem för"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20f%C3%B6r)): Modia targets it ([Modia](https://modia.se/kunskapsbank/affarssystem-for-tillverkande-foretag)).
  - "kravspecifikation affärssystem" ([Google "kravspecifikation affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=kravspecifikation%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)): BrightCom's sitemap lists a page on it ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/business-central-kravspecifikation)).
- Tests named in the task:
  - **"byta från Fortnox till Business Central":** nothing on Google ([Google "byta från fortnox till business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=byta%20fr%C3%A5n%20fortnox%20till%20business%20central)), and Bing shows only unrelated Fortnox account queries ([Bing "byta från fortnox till business central"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=byta%20fr%C3%A5n%20fortnox%20till%20business%20central&mkt=sv-SE)). "byta från fortnox" completes only to small-business tools such as Spiris, Visma and Bokio ([Google "byta från fortnox"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=byta%20fr%C3%A5n%20fortnox)).
  - **"Business Central för grossister / tillverkning / tjänsteföretag":** nothing ([Google "business central för grossister"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20f%C3%B6r%20grossister), [Google "business central för tillverkning"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20f%C3%B6r%20tillverkning), [Google "business central tjänsteföretag"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20tj%C3%A4nstef%C3%B6retag)). The demand is phrased "affärssystem för tillverkande företag / grossister / tjänsteföretag" ([Google "affärssystem för"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20f%C3%B6r), [Google "affärssystem för grossister"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20f%C3%B6r%20grossister), [Google "affärssystem för tjänsteföretag"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=aff%C3%A4rssystem%20f%C3%B6r%20tj%C3%A4nstef%C3%B6retag)).
  - **"Navision support upphör":** nothing ([Google "navision support upphör"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20support%20upph%C3%B6r)). The English lifecycle forms are recognised (above).
  - **"migrera NAV till Business Central":** Google maps it to "migrate nav to business central" ([Google "migrera nav till business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=migrera%20nav%20till%20business%20central)).
  - **"Business Central pris per användare":** Google maps it to "business central price per user" ([Google "business central pris per användare"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20pris%20per%20anv%C3%A4ndare)).
  - **City variants, timpris and partner switching:** "business central partner/konsult stockholm/göteborg/malmö", "business central konsult timpris" and "byta business central partner" got no Google suggestions; Bing returned only unrelated or foreign queries ([Google "business central partner stockholm"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20partner%20stockholm), [Google "business central partner göteborg"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20partner%20g%C3%B6teborg), [Google "business central konsult stockholm"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20konsult%20stockholm), [Google "business central konsult timpris"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20konsult%20timpris), [Google "byta business central partner"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=byta%20business%20central%20partner)).

### Inferences
- Publish the NAV cluster first (service page, end-of-life article, BC vs NAV article), ideally before NAV 2017's end of support on 12 Jan 2027. Use Swedish titles with the English lifecycle phrases in titles and H2s.
- Rephrase the long-tail ideas to the wording searchers actually use:
  - The industry pages target "affärssystem för …".
  - The pricing article includes a "pris per användare" H2.
  - The NAV article combines "end of life" with "supporten upphör".
- Help articles for existing users (SIE, driftstörning) are cheap to produce and feed the support and förvaltning services. Choose topics covered by the Swedish localization and Microsoft documentation, so the content can be verified.
- Do not build Fortnox-to-Business Central or city landing pages now. They have no demand evidence (T4), and city pages would also need verified local presence.

### Gaps
- The competitive picture for the quick wins comes from Bing only. Google's ranking pages for SIE, driftstörning and go-live were not checked.

## 6. Brand collision: what does autocomplete return for "Verdana", and which brand queries should the site own?

### Takeaway
"verdana" belongs to the typeface. In Sweden it also collides with an existing company, Verdana Fond & Försäkring AB (Stockholm), and on Bing with the investor Verdane. Brand plus category combinations ("verdana business central", "verdana konsult", "verdana affärssystem") return nothing today. They are free to own, but they will only capture people who already know the name. Wherever the site identifies itself, it must pair the brand with "Business Central".

### Cited Findings
- Google "verdana": verdana font, verdansk, verdana typsnitt, verdana meaning, verdana font download, verdansk map / dave / bunker codes 2026, verdandi ([Google "verdana"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana)).
- Google alphabet expansion:
  - "verdana fond & försäkring ab" is #5 for "verdana " and #3 for "verdana ab" ([Google "verdana "](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20), [Google "verdana ab"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20ab)).
  - Other completions include verdana dubai and verdana pro ([Google "verdana "](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20)), verdana skrift ([Google "verdana s"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20s)) and verdana kursiv ([Google "verdana k"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20k)). The full list is in section 8.
- Bing:
  - "verdana" returns verdana bold, verdana pro, verdanadesk, verdana village (Estero, FL) and verdana reportage ([Bing "verdana"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=verdana&mkt=sv-SE)).
  - "verdana fond och försäkring" is #6 for "verdana o" ([Bing "verdana o"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=verdana%20o&mkt=sv-SE)).
  - "verdane capital" is #6 for "verdana e" ([Bing "verdana e"](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=verdana%20e&mkt=sv-SE)).
- Brand plus category:
  - "verdana business central" returned only "verdana sales center" ([Google "verdana business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20business%20central)).
  - "verdana affärssystem" ([Google "verdana affärssystem"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20aff%C3%A4rssystem)), "verdana konsult" ([Google "verdana konsult"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20konsult)), "verdana dynamics" ([Google "verdana dynamics"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20dynamics)) and "verdana sverige" ([Google "verdana sverige"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20sverige)) returned nothing on any engine.
  - "verdana consulting" returned itself plus "verdana sfi consulting" ([Google "verdana consulting"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=verdana%20consulting)).
- Bing's results page for "verdana" showed only typeface results: Wikipedia (en/sv), Adobe Fonts, Microsoft Typography and font download sites ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=verdana&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).
- Verdana Fond & Försäkring AB appears in Bing's local results as a financial-services business in Stockholm. hitta.se's result snippet describes it as active in insurance agency and brokerage, and LinkedIn lists the company ([Bing](https://www.bing.com/search?q=%22verdana+fond%22+f%C3%B6rs%C3%A4kring&setlang=sv&cc=SE)).

### Inferences
- Do not target "verdana" alone. Own "Verdana Business Central", "Verdana konsult(er)", "Verdana affärssystem", "Verdana Dynamics 365", "Verdana Navision" and "Verdana ERP" by using brand and category together:
  - in the titles of the homepage, about page and contact page (e.g. "Om Verdana – konsulter inom Business Central")
  - in the first sentence of the homepage and about page
  - in the footer identification line
  - in Organization/WebSite structured data, for example as a descriptive alternate name such as "Verdana Business Central" (check this against Google's current site-name documentation before implementing)
- Avoid wording that could be confused with Verdana Fond & Försäkring AB, such as "Verdana AB" without context, or insurance and finance phrasing. Keep the company's legal name and identifiers consistent once they are confirmed.
- The brand query family is navigational and will grow only with offline and LinkedIn awareness. Measure it in Search Console after launch rather than estimating it now.

### Gaps
- It is unknown whether the final domain will contain a category word, which affects how much the titles must carry.
- Verdana's legal entity name was not checked against Bolagsverket (out of scope and unverified).

## 7. How should the keyword universe map to pages? (deliverables 3–6)

### Takeaway
Build a hub-and-spoke site:
- a homepage that owns "Business Central-konsulter"
- a /business-central hub that owns the product name and the "affärssystem" bridge
- nine service pages (/tjanster/…), four industry pages (/losningar/…) and 13 insight articles (/insikter/…), each with a single primary keyword

Use lowercase, hyphenated slugs with å/ä/ö transliterated to a/a/o. All 33 specified pages have Swedish titles of at most 60 characters and meta descriptions of at most 155 characters, verified by script. None of them depends on unverified partner status, certifications, references, prices or history.

### Cited Findings
- Google's URL guidance ([Google Search Central](https://developers.google.com/search/docs/crawling-indexing/url-structure)):
  - "characters in the non-ASCII range should be percent encoded"; `example.com/gemüse` is listed as not recommended, with `gem%C3%BCse` as the recommended form.
  - Hyphens are recommended instead of underscores.
  - "Use words in your audience's language in the URL (and, if applicable, transliterated words)."
- Swedish competitors transliterate å/ä/ö in slugs:
  - NAB: `/se/branscher/erp-for-grossister/` ([NAB](https://nabsolutions.com/se/branscher/erp-for-grossister/))
  - Modia: `/kunskapsbank/vad-kostar-ett-affarssystem` ([Modia](https://modia.se/kunskapsbank/vad-kostar-ett-affarssystem))
  - BrightCom: `/artiklar/uppgradera-dynamics-nav` ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/artiklar/uppgradera-dynamics-nav))
  - Exsitec mixes transliterated slugs such as `/affarssystem/microsoft-dynamics-365-business-central` ([Exsitec](https://www.exsitec.se/affarssystem/microsoft-dynamics-365-business-central)) with percent-encoded ones such as `/artiklar/automatiserat-aff%C3%A4rssystem` ([Exsitec](https://www.exsitec.se/artiklar/automatiserat-aff%C3%A4rssystem)).
- Competitor title patterns:
  - "Dynamics 365 Business Central-partner i Sverige | BrightCom" ([BrightCom](https://www.brightcom.se/))
  - "Business Central-partner för växande företag | Modia" ([Modia](https://modia.se/))
  - "Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central - Exsitec" ([Exsitec](https://www.exsitec.se/affarssystem/microsoft-dynamics-365-business-central))
  - "Upptäck ett affärssystem som växer med ditt företag – Microsoft ERP" ([NAB](https://nabsolutions.com/se/erp/))
  - Microsoft: "Business Central | Microsoft Dynamics 365" ([Microsoft](https://www.microsoft.com/sv-se/dynamics-365/products/business-central)) and "Prissättning för Business Central | Microsoft Dynamics 365" ([Microsoft](https://www.microsoft.com/sv-se/dynamics-365/products/business-central/pricing))
- Competitor weaknesses:
  - Fetched without JavaScript, Modia's /tjanster and three /kunskapsbank/ URLs returned the homepage's title and meta description instead of page-specific ones ([Modia](https://modia.se/kunskapsbank/vad-kostar-ett-affarssystem)).
  - Fellowmind's Swedish homepage has an English title, "Fellowmind uses technology to create meaningful connections" ([Fellowmind](https://www.fellowmind.com/sv-se/)).
  - Evidi's Swedish-slug article has the English title "Evidi | When Do You Need an ERP? | A Guide for Businesses" ([Evidi](https://www.evidi.com/deliverables/erp-business-management-system/nar-behover-ett-foretag-byta-affarssystem)).
- Project constraints (project file `docs/MASTER-BRIEF.md`, sections 4 and 7):
  - Navigation: Hem, Business Central, Tjänster, Lösningar, Insikter, Om Verdana, Kontakt; primary CTA "Boka rådgivning".
  - Do not invent partner status, certifications, references, results, employee numbers, history, awards or pricing commitments.
  - Industry pages must avoid implying prior industry experience unless it is verified.
  - Product statements must be verified against Microsoft documentation.

### Inferences

#### Slug policy: transliterate å/ä/ö
- Transliterate å→a, ä→a, ö→o (and "&"→"och"), lowercase, with hyphens between words. Examples: `/tjanster/forvaltning`, `/insikter/vad-kostar-business-central`, `/losningar/tjansteforetag`.
- Reasons:
