  1. Google accepts both forms, but raw å/ä/ö become `%C3%A5` etc. when URLs are copied into email, CRM, ads or chat, which hurts readability and trust.
  2. Google explicitly endorses transliterated audience-language words.
  3. The main Swedish competitors transliterate, so the convention is familiar to Swedish buyers.
  4. Keywords in URLs are a minor signal, so readability decides.
- Keep slugs short and stable. Keep years out of evergreen slugs; put the year in the title and update the page instead. Plan English pages under `/en/…` later, with hreflang sv-SE/en.
- The NAV service slug uses the natural Swedish phrase `navision-till-business-central` rather than `migrering`. Swedish "migrering" returns English-only suggestions ([Google "business central migrering"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20migrering)). Google answers "navision till business central" with ten "navision business central" / "navision to business central" completions ([Google "navision till business central"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=navision%20till%20business%20central)), so it matches the phrase to the right intent.

#### Site architecture
```
/                                        Hem (Business Central-konsulter)
/business-central                        Business Central hub
/tjanster                                Tjänster (hub)
  /tjanster/radgivning                   ERP-rådgivning
  /tjanster/implementering               Implementering
  /tjanster/navision-till-business-central  Migrering & modernisering (NAV/Navision + andra system)
  /tjanster/integrationer-och-utveckling Integrationer & utveckling (API, AL, Power Platform)
  /tjanster/support-och-optimering       Support & optimering
  /tjanster/forvaltning                  Förvaltning
  /tjanster/konsulttjanster              Konsulttjänster (inhyrda konsulter)
  /tjanster/rapportering-och-power-bi    Rapportering & Power BI
  /tjanster/ai-och-automation            AI & automation (Copilot)
/losningar                               Lösningar (hub)
  /losningar/tillverkning
  /losningar/handel-och-grossist
  /losningar/e-handel
  /losningar/tjansteforetag
/insikter                                Insikter (hub)
  /insikter/vad-kostar-business-central
  /insikter/business-central-licenser
  /insikter/navision-end-of-life
  /insikter/business-central-vs-navision
  /insikter/byta-affarssystem
  /insikter/kravspecifikation-affarssystem
  /insikter/valja-business-central-partner
  /insikter/business-central-shopify
  /insikter/copilot-i-business-central
  /insikter/sie-export-business-central
  /insikter/business-central-driftstorning
  /insikter/checklista-go-live-business-central
  /insikter/vad-ar-ett-affarssystem
/om-verdana
/kontakt
```
The brief's separate "Business Central Development" page is covered by /tjanster/integrationer-och-utveckling (AL, extensions, APIs), because the development queries are English and overlap with integration intent.

#### Page-by-page specifications (deliverables 3 and 5)
- Character counts are Unicode characters, computed by script.
- Insight-article titles omit "| Verdana" to stay within 60 characters; Google usually shows the site name separately.
- A † after a keyword means it never appears as a Google or Bing suggestion, also when hyphens are treated as spaces. It is kept for natural Swedish wording, navigation or brand reasons, and should not be treated as demand-backed.
- In FAQ evidence, G = Google autocomplete, B = Bing autocomplete, S = another cited source, P = predicted (no direct evidence).

#### Homepage keyword focus and H1/H2 wording (deliverable 4)
- **Primary focus:** "Business Central-konsulter / business central konsult" (Google echo #1, [Google "business central konsult"](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central%20konsult)).
- **Supporting terms:** Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central; affärssystem; implementering; migrering från Navision; integrationer; support och förvaltning.
- **Do not use on the homepage:** "partner", "certifierad", customer counts or prices until they are verified. Use "Business Central-partner" only if Microsoft partner status is confirmed; until then use the neutral "Business Central-konsulter".
- **H1 options:**
  1. "Business Central-konsulter för företag som vill mer med sitt affärssystem" (recommended: primary keyword plus category word).
  2. "Vi hjälper svenska företag att lyckas med Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central" (entity-rich; good if the hero needs the full product name).
  3. "Ett affärssystem som håller när ni växer – Business Central med Verdana" (brand-forward, weaker keyword match).
- **Hero sub-line:** "Rådgivning, implementering, migrering från Navision, integrationer och support för Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central."
- **H2 options, by homepage section in the brief:**
  - Value proposition: "Ett affärssystem som växer med er verksamhet"
  - Services: "Tjänster inom Business Central – från rådgivning till förvaltning"
  - Migration: "Från Navision till Business Central – utan att tappa fart"
  - Ecosystem: "Business Central, Microsoft 365, Power BI och Copilot i samma flöde"
  - Industries: "Affärssystem för tillverkning, handel, e-handel och tjänsteföretag"
  - Implementation journey: "Så går en implementering av Business Central till"
  - Credibility: "Så arbetar vi med Business Central-projekt" (method and principles, not unverified references)
  - Content highlights: "Insikter om Business Central och affärssystem"
  - FAQ: "Vanliga frågor om Business Central"
  - CTA: "Boka rådgivning om Business Central"

#### FAQ guidance (deliverable 5)
- The per-page FAQ items are listed above. Items marked G or B use wording seen in autocomplete. Items marked P are predicted and should not be presented as "what people search".
- Answer each FAQ in two to four sentences and link to the page that owns the topic. Pricing answers must cite Microsoft's list prices with a date and must not state Verdana prices.
- Check Copilot and licensing answers against current Microsoft documentation before publishing, because they change with every release wave.

#### Internal linking (deliverable 6)
1. **Hub and spoke.** /business-central links to every service with descriptive anchors such as "implementering av Business Central" or "migrering från Navision till Business Central". Every service page links back to /business-central and to /kontakt.
2. **Homepage links.** The homepage links to the high-priority set: /business-central, /tjanster/implementering, /tjanster/navision-till-business-central, /tjanster/support-och-optimering, /tjanster/konsulttjanster, /insikter/vad-kostar-business-central and /insikter/navision-end-of-life.
3. **One money page per article.** Each insight article links to exactly one service page as its primary call to action, plus one or two sibling articles:
   - NAV articles → NAV service
   - pricing and licences → implementation and rådgivning
   - byta affärssystem and kravspecifikation → rådgivning
   - Shopify → integrations and e-handel
   - Copilot → AI service
   - SIE and driftstörning → support
   - go-live → implementation
4. **Anchor text.** Use the target page's primary keyword or a close variant, never "läs mer", and vary anchors naturally.
5. **Industry pages.** Link to /business-central, /tjanster/implementering, the relevant integration content (e-handel → Shopify article) and the licensing article (tillverkning → Premium licence questions).
6. **Breadcrumbs and footer.** Use breadcrumbs (Hem › Tjänster › Implementering), ideally with BreadcrumbList markup, and a footer listing the main services and Kontakt.
7. **New content.** When a new article is published, add one or two contextual links to it from older relevant pages so that no page is orphaned. If the brief's planned ERP cost calculator is built, link it from the pricing article and the pricing article from it.
8. **Cannibalisation map** (one owner per primary keyword):
   - business central konsult(er) → homepage; hyra / inhyrd konsult → /tjanster/konsulttjanster
   - business central (affärssystem), vad är business central → /business-central
   - vad kostar business central, business central pris → pricing article; licenser, essentials vs premium → licensing article
   - business central implementering → implementation page; go live checklist → go-live article
   - navision business central / navision till business central → NAV service; navision end of life → lifecycle article; business central vs navision → comparison article
   - erp rådgivning → /tjanster/radgivning; byta affärssystem → article; kravspecifikation → article
   - business central integration → integrations page; business central shopify → Shopify article; affärssystem för e-handel → /losningar/e-handel
   - business central support → support page; förvaltning → förvaltning page; driftstörning and sie export → help articles
   - power bi business central → Power BI page; copilot business central → Copilot article; AI i Business Central → AI service
   - business central partner sverige → partner-choice article, not the homepage, until partner status is verified

#### Suggested publishing order
- **Phase 1** (with the approved homepage): homepage, /business-central, implementering, navision-till-business-central, support-och-optimering, konsulttjanster, om-verdana, kontakt, vad-kostar-business-central and navision-end-of-life.
- **Phase 2:** radgivning, integrationer-och-utveckling, forvaltning, rapportering-och-power-bi, ai-och-automation, business-central-licenser, business-central-vs-navision, byta-affarssystem and valja-business-central-partner.
- **Phase 3:** the /losningar pages, then the Shopify, kravspecifikation, SIE, driftstörning, go-live, vad-är-ett-affärssystem and Copilot articles.
- NAV 2017's end of support (12 Jan 2027) argues for publishing the NAV content in 2026 ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/lifecycle/products/dynamics-nav-2017)).

### Gaps
- Feature claims in the page copy must be verified against current Microsoft documentation when the pages are written: manufacturing in Premium, the Shopify Connector, and Copilot capabilities, language support and licensing. This research verified only Swedish SIE support and the NAV lifecycle dates.
- The /tjanster/forvaltning page has no ERP-specific search demand. It is kept for sales completeness; merging it into support is the SEO-optimal alternative if the business allows.

## 8. Raw harvest: what did each engine return for every seed? (deliverable 1)

### Takeaway
Below is the complete deduplicated harvest from 2026-10-11, grouped by seed and engine.
- Alphabet expansions: the 30 lists per seed are merged and deduplicated per engine.
- DuckDuckGo is summarised against Bing, because it mirrored Bing.
- Each seed links to its own requests. For alphabet sets, the "a" request is linked and the pattern repeats per letter.
- The supplementary seeds (Google and Bing only) come last.

### Cited Findings
- Sources, harvested 2026-10-11 between 13:48 and 14:50 CEST; every entry below links to its own request:
  - Google endpoint: `https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=…` (requested with `ie=utf-8&oe=utf-8` added)
  - Bing endpoint: `https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=…&mkt=sv-SE`
  - DuckDuckGo endpoint: `https://duckduckgo.com/ac/?q=…&kl=se-sv&type=list`

#### Required seeds (minimum list from the task)

- **"business central"** — [Google](https://suggestqueries.google.com/complete/search?client=firefox&hl=sv&gl=se&q=business%20central) · [Bing](https://api.bing.com/osjson.aspx?query=business%20central&mkt=sv-SE) · [DDG](https://duckduckgo.com/ac/?q=business%20central&kl=se-sv&type=list)
  - Google (10): business central · business central 365 · business central dynamics 365 · business central login · business central dynamics · business central microsoft · business central erp · business central mcp · business central api · business central logo

### Inferences
- Read the lists in their original order. The position in each engine's list is the ranking signal used in sections 1–5.
- Treat non-Swedish items (Norwegian NAV, Danish and German phrases, US place names) as noise, not Swedish demand.

### Gaps
- Suggestions change over time. Re-running this seed list against the same endpoints before major content decisions would show drift.
